ABY_descision_category_united_nations = {
	ABY_descision_invite_democratic = {
		priority = 1

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						TAG = COU
						TAG = SHE
						TAG = ARI
						TAG = RED
						TAG = MIL
						TAG = GEH
						TAG = GEM
						TAG = TRM
						TAG = SRT
						TAG = KSP
						TAG = ABY
						TAG = HYA
						TAG = SHA
						TAG = HEU
					}
				}
				has_capitulated = no
				has_government = democratic
				
				is_puppet = no
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ABY
		}

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {

				has_government = democratic
			}
		}

		visible = {
			country_exists = FROM
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				has_capitulated = no
				has_government = democratic
			}
		}

		cost = 50

		days_remove = 30

		fire_only_once = yes

		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				country_event = {
					id = ABY_new_event.47
				}
			}
		}

ABY_descision_category_revolution = {
	ABY_descision_revolution = {
		priority = 5

		icon = generic_political_rally

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				NOT = { tag = ROOT }
				has_capitulated = no
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ABY
		}

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
		}

		visible = {
			country_exists = FROM
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				has_capitulated = no
				any_controlled_state = {
					is_in_home_area = yes
					any_neighbor_state = {
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			ABY = {
				has_country_flag = ABY_flag_expend_revolution
			}
		}

		cost = 25

		fire_only_once = yes

		complete_effect = {
			FROM = {
					add_timed_idea = {
						idea = ABY_revolutionary_unrest
						days = 360
					}
			}
		}
	}
	ABY_descision_communism_war = {
		priority = 5

		icon = GFX_decision_generic_prepare_civil_war

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				NOT = { tag = ROOT }
				has_capitulated = no
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ABY
		}

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				communism > 0.10
			}
		}

		visible = {
			country_exists = FROM
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				has_capitulated = no
				any_controlled_state = {
					is_in_home_area = yes
					any_neighbor_state = {
						is_controlled_by = ROOT
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				communism > 0.05
			}
			ABY = {
				has_country_flag = ABY_flag_expend_revolution
			}
		}

		cost = 50

		fire_only_once = yes

		days_remove = 70

		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					add_ai_strategy = {
						type = prepare_for_war
						id = ROOT
						value = 100
					}
				}
			}
		}

		remove_effect = {
			create_wargoal = {
				type = topple_government
				target = FROM
				expire = 90
			}
		}
	}
}

ABY_descision_category_only_school = { # 제1학원
	ABY_descision_school_millenium = {
        priority = 1

        allowed = {
            tag = ABY
        }

		visible = {
			tag = ABY
			has_country_flag = ABY_flag_only_school
		}

        available = {
			any_state = {
				is_core_of = MIL
				is_controlled_by = ABY
			}
        }

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 150

		days_remove = 30

        remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_1
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_1
					add_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_2
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_2
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_2
					add_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_3
				}
			}
        }
	}
	ABY_descision_school_germany = {
        priority = 1

        allowed = {
            tag = ABY
        }

		visible = {
			tag = ABY
			has_country_flag = ABY_flag_only_school
		}

        available = {
			any_state = {
				is_core_of = GEM
				is_controlled_by = ABY
			}
			any_state = {
				is_core_of = GEH
				is_controlled_by = ABY
			}
			any_state = {
				is_core_of = TRM   
				is_controlled_by = ABY
			}
        }

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 150

		days_remove = 30

        remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_1
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_1
					add_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_2
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_2
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_2
					add_idea = ABY_idea_only_school_3
				}
			}
        }
		
	}
}

    	}

				}
				transfer_state_to = SHA
			}
			SHA = {
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					create_unit = {
						division = "name = \"Shengaikyo Revolutonary\" division_template = \"Halo Infantry Division\" start_experience_factor = 0"
						owner = SHA
						count = 12
					}
				}
				declare_war_on = {
					target = ABY
					type = annex_everything
				}
			}
		}
	}

	ABY_descision_suspecious_move = {
        priority = 0   

        allowed = {
            tag = ABY
        }

		activation = {
			has_country_flag = ABY_flag_new_country
		}

		available = {
			always = no
    	}

		fire_only_once = yes
 
		days_mission_timeout = 360

		timeout_effect = {	
			clr_country_flag = ABY_flag_new_country
			if = {
				limit = {
					democratic < 0.35
					fascism < 0.35
					neutrality < 0.35
				}#아비도스 입헌공화국(파시내전) 
				add_stability = -0.25
				set_popularities = {
					democratic = 50
					neutrality = 50
					fascism = 0
					communism = 0
				}
				#complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_abydos_federal_republic
				hidden_effect = {
    		        load_focus_tree = {
    		            tree = ABY_focus_tree
    		            keep_completed = yes
    		        }
    		        mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
    		    }
				start_civil_war = {
					ideology = fascism 
					size = 0.5
					army_ratio = 0.25
					keep_all_characters = yes
					set_cosmetic_tag = ABY_revolution
				}
			}	
			if = {
				limit = {
					democratic > 0.35
					fascism < 0.35
					neutrality < 0.35
				}#아비도스 연방공화국
				#complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_abydos_federal_republic
				hidden_effect = {
    		        load_focus_tree = {
    		            tree = ABY_focus_tree
    		            keep_completed = yes
    		        }
    		        mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
    		    }
				country_event = {
					id = ABY_new_event.13
				}
			}	
			if = {
				limit = {
					democratic < 0.35
					fascism > 0.35
					neutrality < 0.35
				}#색채의 군단
				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_12_12
				hidden_effect = {
    		        load_focus_tree = {
    		            tree = ABY_focus_tree
    		            keep_completed = yes
    		        }
    		        mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
    		    }
			}	
			if = {
				limit = {
					democratic < 0.35
					fascism < 0.35
					neutrality > 0.35
				}#신성 아비도스 제국
				complete_national_focus = ABY_focus_holy_empire
				hidden_effect = {

ABY_descision_category_invite_balkan = {
	ABY_descision_invite_balkan = {
		priority = 1

		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				exists = yes
				is_in_faction = no
				OR = {
					TAG = YUG
					TAG = ALB
					TAG = ROM
					TAG = BUL
					TAG = GRE
					TAG = TUR
				}
				has_capitulated = no
				is_puppet = no
			}
		}

		allowed = {
			original_tag = ABY
		}

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
		}

		visible = {
			country_exists = FROM
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				is_in_faction = no
				has_capitulated = no
			}
		}

		cost = 50

		target_trigger = {

				has_capitulated = no
				is_puppet = no
			}
		}

			original_tag = ABY
		}

		available = {
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			has_country_flag = ABY_flag_nephthys_economy
			NOT = {has_country_flag = ABY_flag_nephthys_attack}
		}

		visible = {
			country_exists = FROM
			NOT = { has_war_with = FROM }
			FROM = {
				is_in_faction = no
				has_capitulated = no
			}
			has_country_flag = ABY_flag_nephthys_economy
		}

			FROM = {
				add_ideas = {
					ABY_idea_economic_press
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ABY_flag_nephthys_attack
		}

		remove_effect = {
			FROM = {
				remove_ideas = {
					ABY_idea_economic_press
				}
				country_event = {
					id = ABY_new_event.226
				}
			}
			clr_country_flag = ABY_flag_nephthys_attack
		}
